Here are the steps and observations:

1. **Identify All Paths From H to N:**
   - One path: H → E → L → M → J → I → P → O → N
   - Another path: H → E → L → M → J → D → P → O → N
   - Another path: H → B → C → O → N
   - Another path: H → B → C → P → O → N
   - Another path: H → B → C → I → P → O → N
   - Another path: H → B → C → I → J → D → P → O → N

2. **Identify Critical Nodes Required to Break All Paths between H and N:**
   - Removing I from the graph will interrupt these paths:
     - H → E → L → M → J → I → P → O → N
     - H → E → L → M → J → D → P → O → N
     - H → B → C → I → P → O → N
     - H → B → C → I → J → D → P → O → N
   - Removing O from the graph will interrupt these paths:
     - H → B → C → O → N
     - H → B → C → P → O → N
     - H → B → C → I → P → O → N
     - H → E → L → M → J → I → P → O → N

To determine the minimum number of edges that must be removed so that the two black nodes F and J are completely disconnected, we need to find all possible paths between F and J and identify the critical edges whose removal will eliminate all paths.

### Step-by-Step Analysis:

1. **Identify Node Connections:**
   - Node F is connected to nodes: G, K, X, H, B, and E.
   - Node J is connected to nodes: P, D, I, H, B, and C.

2. **Paths Between F and J:**
   - Path 1: F -> H -> B -> C -> O -> N -> B -> H -> I -> J
   - Path 2: F -> H -> I -> J
   - Path 3: F -> H -> B -> C -> J
   - Path 4: F -> E -> H -> B -> C -> J
   - Path 5: F -> E -> H -> I -> J

### Critical Analysis:

To disconnect nodes F and J, we need to cut off all the possible paths:

- **Path 1 requires breaking:**
  - (F, H) or (H, B) or (B, C) or (H, I) or (I, J).

- **Path 2 requires breaking:**
  - (F, H) or (H, I) or (I, J).

- **Path 3 requires breaking:**
  - (F, H) or (H, B) or (B, C) or (C, J).

- **Path 4 requires breaking:**
  - (F, E) or (E, H) or (H, B) or (B, C).

- **Path 5 requires breaking:**
  - (F, E) or (E, H).

From these paths, we determine that breaking any two of the edges: (F, H), (H, I) or (I, J) will completely disconnect the two black nodes while minimizing the number of edges to be removed.

### Solution:
```plaintext
Edges to remove: 2, Cut Set: [(F, H), (H, I)] or [(F, H), (I, J)]
```
